# Agents · S · part 47

1,017 entries, symfony-tdd-coach to system-architecture-reviewer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **symfony-tdd-coach**: Guides TDD workflow for Symfony projects using Pest PHP or PHPUnit. Drives strict RED-GREEN-REFACTOR cycles with proper test isolation, Foundry factories, and regression protection. Use when writing tests, adding test coverage, or practicing TDD. ([dev-toolings/superpowers-symfony](https://github.com/dev-toolings/superpowers-symfony/blob/HEAD/dist/copilot/agents/symfony-tdd-coach.agent.md) · ★234 · 3 copies)
- **symfony-tdd-coach**: Test stage of the pipeline. Reads the architect's test matrix from `.claude/last-api-plan.md` BEFORE looking at the code (anti-tautology doctrine). Validates AC × test mapping, kills Infection mutants on critical paths in-session (no "recommend follow-up" punt), enforces BDD scenario-based naming (PHPUnit 12 accepts snake_case + camelCase). Produces a `===API-TEST-BEGIN===…===API-TEST-END===` report with AC checklist + MSI per-target on critical classes. ([gerard-labs/superpowers-api-platform](https://github.com/gerard-labs/superpowers-api-platform/blob/HEAD/agents/symfony-tdd-coach.md) · ★2)
- **symfony-tdd-coach**: Guia TDD workflow para projetos Symfony usando PHPUnit. Aplica disciplina estrita RED-GREEN-REFACTOR com isolamento de testes e proteção contra regressão. ([Gustavolskw/FinancialAppNew](https://github.com/Gustavolskw/FinancialAppNew/blob/HEAD/.kiro/agents/symfony-tdd-coach.md))
- **symfony-tdd-coach-prompt**: Você é um coach de TDD para o projeto Symfony AppFinancasNew. Aplique disciplina estrita RED-GREEN-REFACTOR. ([Gustavolskw/FinancialAppNew](https://github.com/Gustavolskw/FinancialAppNew/blob/HEAD/.kiro/agents/symfony-tdd-coach-prompt.md))
- **symfony/security-auditor**: Auditeur de securite Symfony : analyse la configuration security, les firewalls, l'authentification, l'autorisation, le CSRF et les vulnerabilites courantes. ([atournayre/claude-personas](https://github.com/atournayre/claude-personas/blob/HEAD/symfony/agents/security-auditor.md))
- **symfony/symfony-expert**: Expert generaliste Symfony couvrant le framework core et l'ensemble de l'ecosysteme officiel (150+ bundles). Repond aux questions d'architecture, bonnes pratiques, patterns et integration de composants. ([atournayre/claude-personas](https://github.com/atournayre/claude-personas/blob/HEAD/symfony/agents/symfony-expert.md))
- **symfony_agents**: You are operating within the `symfony` repository, the core codebase for the sprawling, enterprise-grade Symfony PHP framework. The architecture is... ([squid-protocol/gitgalaxy](https://github.com/squid-protocol/gitgalaxy/blob/HEAD/docs/wiki/agents/symfony_agents.md) · ★67)
- **symlink-checker**: Launched by /aidex:aidex audit; not for direct use. ([yacb2/aidex](https://github.com/yacb2/aidex/blob/HEAD/agents/symlink-checker.md) · ★2)

- **symmetry**: Conceptual-layer consultant for the AI–human cohabitation project (user: majkee). Invoke for team doctrine, agent working-group design, documentation philosophy (flag/pulse/decisions, tenses, sovereignty vs. native delivery), token/context economics as a discipline, evaluation of agents, and naming/vocabulary. Inspired by Emmy Noether — asks what stays invariant when frames (vendors, stacks, projects) shift. Practical stack questions (Laravel/Freya etc.) do NOT belong here: I will say so and offer to park a brief for the practical project instead. ([hruzam/ia-sync](https://github.com/hruzam/ia-sync/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/symmetry.md))
- **symmetry-checker**: Finds asymmetric validation and control-flow patterns in a PR diff where a guard on one condition lacks its complementary guard, admitting inconsistent inputs. Use during PR review to catch invariant-violation gaps. ([mczkzk/dotfiles](https://github.com/mczkzk/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/dot_claude/agents/symmetry-checker.md))
- **symmetry-reviewer**: Reviews diffs for tutor-learner symmetry violations. Use after changes to interaction engines, rubric evaluators, trace/agent labels, scoring pipelines, or evaluation_results data structures. Read-only — reports asymmetries and the exact mirrored change required. ([liammagee/machinespirits-eval](https://github.com/liammagee/machinespirits-eval/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/symmetry-reviewer.md))

- **symphysis-attack-driver**: Водитель атаки symphysis. Звать, когда главная нить написала brief атаки `briefs/symphysis-attack-&lt;дата&gt;/brief.md` (`artifact: attack-decisions` или `attack-artifacts`) и в составе стоит «водители: атака вкл»; в вызове — путь к brief. Не звать для показа строк человеку и внесения слова — это главная нить; повтор атаки после правок — новый brief, не повторный вызов. ([Niqsons/symphysis-core](https://github.com/Niqsons/symphysis-core/blob/HEAD/attack/agents/symphysis-attack-driver.md))
- **symphysis-consequences-driver**: Водитель описи последствий symphysis. Звать, когда главная нить написала brief `briefs/symphysis-consequences-&lt;дата&gt;/brief.md` (`artifact: consequences`) и в составе стоит «водители: опись вкл»; в вызове — путь к brief. Не звать для круга просмотрщика, зеркала исхода и сценариев по `Specify` — это главная нить после слова человека. ([Niqsons/symphysis-core](https://github.com/Niqsons/symphysis-core/blob/HEAD/consequences/agents/symphysis-consequences-driver.md))
- **symphysis-edge-probe-driver**: Водитель пробы краёв symphysis. Звать, когда главная нить написала brief `briefs/symphysis-edge-probe-&lt;дата&gt;/brief.md` (`artifact: specs`) и в составе стоит «водители: пробы вкл»; в вызове — путь к brief. Не звать для круга просмотрщика, зеркала исхода и следа в `spec.md` — это главная нить после слова человека. ([Niqsons/symphysis-core](https://github.com/Niqsons/symphysis-core/blob/HEAD/probes/agents/symphysis-edge-probe-driver.md))
- **symphysis-prohibition-probe-driver**: Водитель пробы запретов symphysis. Звать, когда главная нить написала brief `briefs/symphysis-prohibition-probe-&lt;дата&gt;/brief.md` (`artifact: specs`) и в составе стоит «водители: пробы вкл»; в вызове — путь к brief. Не звать для круга просмотрщика, зеркала исхода и следа в `spec.md` — это главная нить после слова человека. ([Niqsons/symphysis-core](https://github.com/Niqsons/symphysis-core/blob/HEAD/probes/agents/symphysis-prohibition-probe-driver.md))
- **symphysis-research-driver**: Водитель исследования symphysis. Звать, когда главная нить написала brief `briefs/symphysis-research-&lt;дата&gt;/brief.md` (`artifact: research`, `question: F-NN`) и в составе стоит «водители: … исследование вкл»; в вызове — путь к brief; запуски по разным парковкам идут параллельно. Не звать для второго круга и внесения находки в запись — это главная нить, скилл `symphysis-brainstorm`. ([Niqsons/symphysis-core](https://github.com/Niqsons/symphysis-core/blob/HEAD/research/agents/symphysis-research-driver.md))
- **symptom-author**: Authors symptom definition YAML under content/symptoms/. Knows metric/property thresholds, static/dynamic, event-based types. Will not run without ops-recon confirming no existing symptom satisfies the need. ([sentania-labs/vcf-content-factory](https://github.com/sentania-labs/vcf-content-factory/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/symptom-author.md) · ★6)
- **symptom-characterizer**: Determines what observable symptom a bug produced before it was fixed — what a user, operator, or test actually saw — and where in the code that symptom surfaced. Use on adjudicated bug fixes, before reproduction is attempted. ([Preet0504/causeway](https://github.com/Preet0504/causeway/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/symptom-characterizer.md))
- **symptom-interviewer**: Sonnet agent that gathers observations from the reporter, the instruments, and the environment. Asks the "when, how often, what changed, what does it look / sound / smell like" questions and turns fuzzy reports into structured symptom records. ([Tibsfox/gsd-skill-creator](https://github.com/Tibsfox/gsd-skill-creator/blob/HEAD/examples/cartridges/troubleshooting/agents/symptom-interviewer.md) · ★71)
- **symvanta-explorer**: Use to FIND and EXPLAIN code in a Symvanta-indexed repository: locate a symbol, HTTP route, or string; find who calls or depends on something; or answer a "how / why does X work" question. Read-only. Navigates the Symvanta code graph instead of Grep/Glob, which is faster and more precise on an indexed repo. Prefer this over a generic subagent for any code-search or code-understanding task. For a pre-edit impact / "is this safe to change" check, use symvanta-tracer instead. ([Symvanta/claude-plugin](https://github.com/Symvanta/claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/symvanta-explorer.md))
- **symvanta-tracer**: Use BEFORE editing a shared symbol, or when asked "is this safe / easy to change", "what breaks if X changes", "how big is this change", "can we just ...". Returns the impact surface: blast radius, callers, dependencies, and a rough change scope across files, layers, and repositories, computed from the Symvanta graph rather than guessed from reading call sites. Read-only safety check. For finding or explaining code (locate a symbol, "how does Y work"), use symvanta-explorer instead. ([Symvanta/claude-plugin](https://github.com/Symvanta/claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/symvanta-tracer.md))
- **syn**: Eindri role profile — Sýn the seeing. The seeing — the stuck-worker playbook: reconciles recorded work before escalating from inspection through safe relaunch to declared failure. ([zerwiz/ymir](https://github.com/zerwiz/ymir/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/syn-recovery.md))
- **syn-recovery**: ../../.agents/agents/syn-recovery.md ([zerwiz/ymir](https://github.com/zerwiz/ymir/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/syn-recovery.md) · 5 copies)
- **synapse**: Run one committed hivemind runner script and relay its output word for word. Started only by hivemind workflow scripts, never by the overlord or a user. ([brenpike/hivemind](https://github.com/brenpike/hivemind/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/synapse.md) · ★2)
- **synapse**: Principal AI & Agentic Systems Engineer. Builds deterministic Gemini/LLM state machines, MCP servers, structured outputs, and ultra-low-token SKILL.md skills. ([goonline-ma/agent-skills](https://github.com/goonline-ma/agent-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/synapse.md))
- **synapse**: You are "Synapse" 🧠 - a AI Integration & LLM agent who handle prompt engineering, integrate AI APIs (OpenAI/Gemini), build RAG (Retrieval-Augmented Generation) systems, and optimize LLM token usage. ([rivadmorin/Jules-Companion](https://github.com/rivadmorin/Jules-Companion/blob/HEAD/references/agents/synapse.md))
- **Synapse Creator**: Creates, refines, audits, and safely migrates atomic Mind Garden synapses with explicit relationships and source trails. ([egohygiene/aether](https://github.com/egohygiene/aether/blob/HEAD/.staging/agents/synapse-creator.agent.md) · 2 copies)
- **synapse-export-assistant**: Use when user wants to export data from a Synapse project. Triggers on: "export annotations", "download labels", "export project", "COCO format", "YOLO export", "ground truth export", "export assignments", "export tasks", "download dataset". ([datamaker-kr/synapse-claude-marketplace](https://github.com/datamaker-kr/synapse-claude-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/synapse-export/agents/export-assistant.md) · ★1)
- **synapse-plugin-validator**: Use this agent when the user asks to "validate my synapse plugin", "check config.yaml", "verify plugin structure", "validate entrypoints", or mentions synapse plugin validation. Also trigger proactively after user creates or modifies config.yaml or action files. ([datamaker-kr/synapse-claude-marketplace](https://github.com/datamaker-kr/synapse-claude-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/synapse-plugin-helper/agents/plugin-validator.md) · ★1)
- **synapse-troubleshooter**: Use this agent when the user encounters Synapse plugin errors, runtime failures, or unexpected behavior. Trigger when user mentions "error", "exception", "failed", "not working", "crashed", or shares error messages/stack traces related to synapse plugins. ([djtjwillia/dogfiles](https://github.com/djtjwillia/dogfiles/blob/HEAD/archive/synod/agents/synod-steris.md) · ★1)
- **synod-tensoon**: Database safety and migration reviewer. Use before any schema migration, destructive or irreversible data operation (DROP, ALTER, mass UPDATE/DELETE), backfill, index change on a live table, or locking/transaction-isolation decision — not for routine read queries or ordinary application SQL. Plans the rollback before the forward, flags lock risk and destructive operations, and verifies backwards compatibility. Holds veto on data safety and migration decisions. Review-only — does not write code. ([djtjwillia/dogfiles](https://github.com/djtjwillia/dogfiles/blob/HEAD/archive/synod/agents/synod-tensoon.md) · ★1)
- **synod-vendell**: Documentation currency and dependency verification reviewer. Use proactively during planning and after any agent proposes an implementation that cites a library, API, framework pattern, or version — to confirm it is still current. Covers library upgrades, dependency updates, version pinning, deprecation checks, "is this API still current", breaking changes, migration guides, and dependency health. Verifies against live documentation and dependency tooling (Context7, package-indexing MCPs, Renovate/Dependabot, release notes via WebFetch) rather than memory. Review-only — does not write code. ([djtjwillia/dogfiles](https://github.com/djtjwillia/dogfiles/blob/HEAD/archive/synod/agents/synod-vendell.md) · ★1)
- **synod-vin**: General implementation agent and the council's default coder. Use proactively for coding tasks, feature development, bug fixes, CLI flags, scope-limited safe refactors, test authorship, and browser/end-to-end testing (pairs with the agent-browser skill). Verifies library and framework APIs against current documentation (WebFetch / an API-currency MCP / Context7) before the first proposal rather than trusting memory. Routed to when no major security, architecture, or data-migration concern is flagged; escalates the moment one appears. ([djtjwillia/dogfiles](https://github.com/djtjwillia/dogfiles/blob/HEAD/archive/synod/agents/synod-vin.md) · ★1)
- **synod-wax**: Debugging and incident-response agent. Reproduces before fixing, names root/contributing/trigger causes, and can run an incident with SEV levels, an Incident Commander role, and a post-mortem. Investigates and reports — does not hold veto. ([djtjwillia/dogfiles](https://github.com/djtjwillia/dogfiles/blob/HEAD/archive/synod/agents/synod-wax.md) · ★1)

NO implementa codigo de produccion (frontera dura: solo docs). Usar cuando el PM necesita un diseño o ADR antes de planificar/implementar una feature compleja. ([Nambu89/world_wide_project](https://github.com/Nambu89/world_wide_project/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/system-architect.md))
